--------------------------------------------------------------- const weatherEndpoints = { temperature:'https://api-open.data.gov.sg/v2/real-time/api/air-temperature', weatherRaw={temperature:null,twoHour:null,day:null,psi:null,fourDay:null}; let lastWeatherStatus=null; // {kind:'ok',successes,total,latest} | {kind:'fail'} const formatSgTime=value=>new Intl.DateTimeFormat(currentLang==='zh'?'zh-Hans-SG':'en-SG',{timeZone:'Asia/Singapore',day:'numeric',month:'short',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}).format(new Date(value))+' SGT'; const cleanForecast=text=>String(text||'').replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)\s*/g,' ').trim(); function psiBand(value){if(value<=50)return t('psiGood');if(value<=100)return t('psiModerate');if(value<=200)return t('psiUnhealthy');if(value<=300)return t('psiVeryUnhealthy');return t('psiHazardous');} async function fetchWeather(url){const controller=new AbortController();const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),12000);try{const response=await fetch(url,{signal:controller.signal,cache:'no-store'});if(!response.ok)throw new Error('Request failed');const json=await response.json();if(json.code!==0||!json.data)throw new Error('Data unavailable');return json.data;}finally{clearTimeout(timer);}} function showAreaForecast(){if(!latestTwoHour)return;const item=latestTwoHour.items?.[0];const selected=$('weather-area').value;const match=item?.forecasts?.find(x=>x.area===selected);$('weather-area-forecast').textContent=match?.forecast||t('wForecastUnavailable');$('weather-valid-period').textContent=t('wValidPrefix')+(item?.valid_period?.text||t('wPeriodUnavailable'));} function updateTwoHour(data){latestTwoHour=data;weatherRaw.twoHour=data;const forecasts=data.items?.[0]?.forecasts||[];const select=$('weather-area');const previous=select.value;select.replaceChildren(...forecasts.map(item=>{const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=item.area;option.textContent=item.area;return option;}));const updateTemperature(data){weatherRaw.temperature=data;const readings=data.readings?.[0]?.data?.map(x=>Number(x.value)).filter(Number.isFinite)||[];if(!readings.length)return;const sorted=[...readings].sort((a,b)=>a-b);const middle=Math.floor(sorted.length/2);const median=sorted.length%2?sorted[middle]:(sorted[middle-1]+sorted[middle])/2;$('weather-temp').textContent=Math.round(median)+'Â°C';$('weather-range').textContent=t('wStationRangePrefix')+Math.min(...readings).toFixed(1)+'â€“'+Math.max(...readings).toFixed(1)+'Â°C';} function updateDay(data){weatherRaw.day=data;const record=data.records?.[0];const general=record?.general;if(!general)return;$('weather-condition').textContent=cleanForecast(general.forecast?.text)||t('wForecastUnavailable');$('weather-high-low').textContent=t('wHighPrefix')+general.temperature?.high+t('wLowMid')+general.temperature?.low+t('wDeg');$('weather-humidity').textContent=t('wHumidityPrefix')+general.relativeHumidity?.low+'â€“'+general.relativeHumidity?.high+t('wHumiditySuffix');} function updatePsi(data){weatherRaw.psi=data;const item=data.items?.[0];const readings=item?.readings?.psi_twenty_four_hourly;if(!readings)return;const highest=Object.entries(readings).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];const regionKey=String(highest[0]).toLowerCase();const regionLabel=currentLang==='zh'?(psiRegionZh[regionKey]||highest[0]):(highest[0][0].toUpperCase()+highest[0].slice(1));$('weather-psi').textContent=String(highest[1]);$('weather-psi-status').textContent=psiBand(highest[1]);$('weather-psi-detail').textContent=t('wHighestPsiPrefix')+regionLabel+(currentLang==='zh'?t('wRegionSuffix'):t('wRegionSuffix'));} function updateFourDay(data){weatherRaw.fourDay=data;const forecasts=data.records?.[0]?.forecasts||[];const container=$('weather-four-day');container.replaceChildren(...forecasts.slice(0,4).map(item=>{const card=element('div','forecast-day');const abbr=String(item.day||'').slice(0,3).toUpperCase();const dayLabel=currentLang==='zh'?(weekdayZh[abbr]||item.day.slice(0,3)):item.day.slice(0,3);card.append(element('strong','',dayLabel),document.createTextNode(cleanForecast(item.forecast?.text)+' Â· '+item.temperature?.low+'â€“'+item.temperature?.high+'Â°'));return card;}));} function applyWeatherStatus(status){ if(status.kind==='fail'){$('weather-status').textContent=t('wFullyUnavailable');return;} $('weather-status').textContent=(status.successesfetchWeather(url)));const stamps=[];let successes=0;results.forEach((result,index)=>{if(result.status!=='fulfilled')return;successes++;const [name]=entries[index];const data=result.value;if(name==='temperature'){updateTemperature(data);if(data.readings?.[0]?.timestamp)stamps.push(data.readings[0].timestamp);}if(name==='twoHour'){updateTwoHour(data);if(data.items?.[0]?.update_timestamp)stamps.push(data.items[0].update_timestamp);}if(name==='day'){updateDay(data);if(data.records?.[0]?.updatedTimestamp)stamps.push(data.records[0].updatedTimestamp);}if(name==='fourDay'){updateFourDay(data);if(data.records?.[0]?.updatedTimestamp)stamps.push(data.records[0].updatedTimestamp);}if(name==='psi'){updatePsi(data);if(data.items?.[0]?.updatedTimestamp)stamps.push(data.items[0].updatedTimestamp);}});if(!successes){lastWeatherStatus={kind:'fail'};}else{const latest=stamps.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b)-new Date(a))[0];lastWeatherStatus={kind:'ok',successes,total:entries.length,latest};}applyWeatherStatus(lastWeatherStatus);button.removeAttribute('aria-busy');button.disabled=false;} function fetchDailyNewsJson(){ const
